问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 的 VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：U1700 的每个关键引脚连接的网络如下（基于证据[1]中完整的 pin mapping 列出所有引脚，因未明确“关键引脚”定义，此处提供全部引脚连接）： - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC5V0 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND 支撑结论的证据： [1] 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF 中 U1700 的 pin mapping 记录（记录 ID 21），明确列出了每个引脚名称及其对应的网络名称。 [2] 同一源文件中 U1700 的实例信息（LP87702-Q1）确认了器件型号。
评分正文：U1700 的每个关键引脚连接的网络如下（基于证据[1]中完整的 pin mapping 列出所有引脚，因未明确“关键引脚”定义，此处提供全部引脚连接）： - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC5V0 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND 支撑结论的证据： [1] 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF 中 U1700 的 pin mapping 记录（记录 ID 21），明确列出了每个引脚名称及其对应的网络名称。 [2] 同一源文件中 U1700 的实例信息（LP87702-Q1）确认了器件型号。

问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：基于提供证据，网络VCC3V3上连接的主要芯片如下： - **U1500 (LN10046)**：证据[1]显示U1500的VIN引脚连接至VCC3V3；证据[8]和[10]说明该芯片为KL30转VCC3V3的DC-DC转换器，是VCC3V3的电源输入端。 - **U1600 (LN10046)**：证据[1]显示U1600的VCCIN引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **U1700 (LN10046)**：证据[1]显示U1700的VIN_B0_1, VIN_B1_1, VANA, VIN_B0_2, VIN_B1_2引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **U1800**：证据[1]显示U1800的VIN和MODE/SYNC引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **U1802**：证据[1]显示U1802的VIN引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **U1804**：证据[1]显示U1804的VIN引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **LP87702**：证据[8]和[11]描述该PMIC将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50。 - **TPS22918**：证据[8]、[13]和[15]描述该负载开关将VCC3V3转换为VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_ETH。 - **SA23002**：证据[9]和[14]描述该电源芯片将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR。 - **TPS62872**：证据[8]和[13]描述该电源转换芯片将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75。 **支撑证据**： - 电路网表证据[1]直接列出了连接到VCC3V3网络的器件及其引脚，包括U1500, U1600, U1700, U1800, U1802, U1804等。 - 文档证据[8]、[10]、[11]、[13]、[15]、[9]、[14]分别描述了芯片LN10046、LP87702、TPS22918、SA23002、TPS62872的功能，并以VCC3V3作为输入电源。 **缺失信息**：无缺失信息。
评分正文：基于提供证据，网络VCC3V3上连接的主要芯片如下： - **U1500 (LN10046)**：证据[1]显示U1500的VIN引脚连接至VCC3V3；证据[8]和[10]说明该芯片为KL30转VCC3V3的DC-DC转换器，是VCC3V3的电源输入端。 - **U1600 (LN10046)**：证据[1]显示U1600的VCCIN引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **U1700 (LN10046)**：证据[1]显示U1700的VIN_B0_1, VIN_B1_1, VANA, VIN_B0_2, VIN_B1_2引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **U1800**：证据[1]显示U1800的VIN和MODE/SYNC引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **U1802**：证据[1]显示U1802的VIN引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **U1804**：证据[1]显示U1804的VIN引脚连接至VCC3V3。 - **LP87702**：证据[8]和[11]描述该PMIC将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50。 - **TPS22918**：证据[8]、[13]和[15]描述该负载开关将VCC3V3转换为VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_ETH。 - **SA23002**：证据[9]和[14]描述该电源芯片将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR。 - **TPS62872**：证据[8]和[13]描述该电源转换芯片将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75。 **支撑证据**： - 电路网表证据[1]直接列出了连接到VCC3V3网络的器件及其引脚，包括U1500, U1600, U1700, U1800, U1802, U1804等。 - 文档证据[8]、[10]、[11]、[13]、[15]、[9]、[14]分别描述了芯片LN10046、LP87702、TPS22918、SA23002、TPS62872的功能，并以VCC3V3作为输入电源。 **缺失信息**：无缺失信息。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. 问题：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含哪些模块电路？
参考答案：HSI 2.3 节列出 KL30 电源管理、唤醒、电源、TC377 最小系统、EQ6 最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗、温度采样、系统内电源电压采样、ETH、CAN/CAN FD 和 3V3 过压保护等电路。
实际回答：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构部分包含以下12个模块电路。 - **KL30电源管理电路** - **唤醒电路** - **电源电路** - **TC377最小系统** - **EQ6最小系统** - **摄像头最小系统** - **看门狗电路** - **温度采样电路** - **系统内电源电压采样电源** - **ETH电路** - **CAN/CAN FD电路** - **3V3过压保护电路** **支撑证据**：文档“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”的“2.3 模块电路”章节，明确列出了上述12个模块电路。
评分正文：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构部分包含以下12个模块电路。 - **KL30电源管理电路** - **唤醒电路** - **电源电路** - **TC377最小系统** - **EQ6最小系统** - **摄像头最小系统** - **看门狗电路** - **温度采样电路** - **系统内电源电压采样电源** - **ETH电路** - **CAN/CAN FD电路** - **3V3过压保护电路** **支撑证据**：文档“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”的“2.3 模块电路”章节，明确列出了上述12个模块电路。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC. the PCB layout is near the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, the bidirectional TVS tube is added to the back end for transient overvoltage protection, the Schottky diode is added vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. When VCC3V3 <4.03V, the cathode pin is in the high resistance state. 问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是 **LP87702**。它输出 **VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50** 三路电压，软件通过 **MCU 的 GPIO 和 I2C 接口**进行配置。 ### 支撑证据 1. **主 PMIC 型号：** * 证据[16]（文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）明确说明：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 * 证据[10]（同一文档）也指出：“VCC1V8...VCC1V1...VCC50...现选择LP87702 PMIC方案”。 2. **输出电压：** * 证据[16] 和 [10] 均明确指出该 PMIC 的输出电压为 **VCC1V8**、**VCC1V1** 和 **VCC50**。 3. **软件配置方式：** * **VCC50 使能控制：** 证据[16] 和 [10] 指出，由 MCU 的 **P15.3** 引脚控制该芯片的 5V 输出。 * **VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 配置：** 证据[16] 和 [10] 指出，通过 MCU 的 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** 和 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 引脚进行 **I2C 通信**，来设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 的输出电压和使能。 * **芯片复位：** 证据[16] 指出，使用 MCU 的 **P12.1** 引脚复位该芯片。 * **寄存器配置：** 证据[15] 提到“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”，但该证据片段未提供配置寄存器的具体列表或值。
评分正文：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是 **LP87702**。它输出 **VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50** 三路电压，软件通过 **MCU 的 GPIO 和 I2C 接口**进行配置。 ### 支撑证据 1. **主 PMIC 型号：** * 证据[16]（文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）明确说明：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 * 证据[10]（同一文档）也指出：“VCC1V8...VCC1V1...VCC50...现选择LP87702 PMIC方案”。 2. **输出电压：** * 证据[16] 和 [10] 均明确指出该 PMIC 的输出电压为 **VCC1V8**、**VCC1V1** 和 **VCC50**。 3. **软件配置方式：** * **VCC50 使能控制：** 证据[16] 和 [10] 指出，由 MCU 的 **P15.3** 引脚控制该芯片的 5V 输出。 * **VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 配置：** 证据[16] 和 [10] 指出，通过 MCU 的 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** 和 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 引脚进行 **I2C 通信**，来设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 的输出电压和使能。 * **芯片复位：** 证据[16] 指出，使用 MCU 的 **P12.1** 引脚复位该芯片。 * **寄存器配置：** 证据[15] 提到“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”，但该证据片段未提供配置寄存器的具体列表或值。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | 数量统计：4; ID：86; SM Name：Processor mechanism for SPF; SM TSR：The ECU shall integrate the processing platform safety mechanism for SPF._x000D_ When the processor SPF is confirmed, the ECU shall:_x000D_ 1. stop outputing ADAS request and;_x000D_ 2. disable the CAN communication and;_x000D_ 3. reset processing platform._x000D_ FHTI < 300 ms._x000D_ When the processor SPF is no longer detected after the processor reset, the ECU shall clear the processor SPF. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_006; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：In initialization process, the SW shall configure SMU reset type to system reset. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_018; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：The SW shall enable the lockstep. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_020; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：软件应该避免PLFASH中出现全0或者全1。如果可能出现全0情况，软件应该使用0x36填充，不能写全1填充。 ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_021; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：软件应保证受CPU0 ENDINIT保护的寄存器被写操作时，正确解开CPU ENDINIT保护，写完寄存器后，再次锁上CPU ENDINIT保护。 解开CPU ENDINIT保护前，软件应屏蔽中断，防止写寄存器时间超时。 解开CPU ENDINIT保护后，写寄存器的时间小于1us ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_016; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：After SMU reset, the SW shall read the SMU fault status register and store SMU fault DTC and Snapshot in NVM. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; 问题：VCC0V75 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：**直接结论：** - VCC0V75 电源由 **TI 的 TPS62872 芯片**产生。 - 该芯片的输入电源是 **VCC3V3**。 - 文档中的配置要求：通过 MCU 的 **P22.0 引脚**（网络名 MCU_VCC0V75_EN）使能芯片，并通过 **MCU_SDA_P15.5 和 MCU_SCL_P15.4 引脚**的 I2C 通信设置输出电压和通信。 **支撑证据：** 1. **产生芯片与输入电源** - 文档明确记载：“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75…现选型 TI 的方案 TPS62872” 以及 “采用 TPS62872 电源芯片，将 VCC3V3 转换为 VCC0V75”【17】【18】。 - 因此输入为 VCC3V3，输出为 VCC0V75。 2. **配置要求** - 同一文档说明：“用 MCU 的 P22.0 引脚使能芯片，用 MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4 引脚 I2C 通信来设置输出电压和通信”【17】【18】。 - 电路图中网络 **MCU_VCC0V75_EN** 连接了 U900E 的 P22.0 引脚，对应使能信号【1】。 - 注意：网络 **MCU_VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN** 连接的是另一颗芯片 SA23002（用于 VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR），与 VCC0V75 无关【2】【19】。 **缺失信息：** 无。所有子问题均被覆盖，无冲突。
评分正文：**直接结论：** - VCC0V75 电源由 **TI 的 TPS62872 芯片**产生。 - 该芯片的输入电源是 **VCC3V3**。 - 文档中的配置要求：通过 MCU 的 **P22.0 引脚**（网络名 MCU_VCC0V75_EN）使能芯片，并通过 **MCU_SDA_P15.5 和 MCU_SCL_P15.4 引脚**的 I2C 通信设置输出电压和通信。 **支撑证据：** 1. **产生芯片与输入电源** - 文档明确记载：“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75…现选型 TI 的方案 TPS62872” 以及 “采用 TPS62872 电源芯片，将 VCC3V3 转换为 VCC0V75”【17】【18】。 - 因此输入为 VCC3V3，输出为 VCC0V75。 2. **配置要求** - 同一文档说明：“用 MCU 的 P22.0 引脚使能芯片，用 MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4 引脚 I2C 通信来设置输出电压和通信”【17】【18】。 - 电路图中网络 **MCU_VCC0V75_EN** 连接了 U900E 的 P22.0 引脚，对应使能信号【1】。 - 注意：网络 **MCU_VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN** 连接的是另一颗芯片 SA23002（用于 VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR），与 VCC0V75 无关【2】【19】。 **缺失信息：** 无。所有子问题均被覆盖，无冲突。

P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable chip, MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set output voltage and communication. +</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Table9-5.DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoints</caption> <tr><td >VSEL Pin(1)</td><td >Device Number</td><td >VSET[7:0]</td><td >Output Voltage Setpoint</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287x-Q1</td><td >0x5a</td><td >850 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY0-Q1</td><td >0x50</td><td >800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY1-Q1</td><td >0x14</td><td >500 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY2-Q1</td><td >0x64</td><td >1800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td >6.2 kQ to GND</td><td >TPS6287xY3-Q1</td><td >0x52</td><td >810 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN0-Q1</td><td >0x14</td><td >500 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN1-Q1</td><td >0x50</td><td >800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN2-Q1</td><td >0x64</td><td >1800 mV</td></tr> </table> | VOUT(VIN - VoUT IL(PP) = VU(NxLxW) | <table><caption> Device Information</caption> <tr><td colspan=3 rowspan=3 >Part Number Current Rating Package TPS62870-Q1 6A TPS62871-Q1 9A RXS (VQFN-FCRLF, TPS62872-Q1 12 A 16) TPS62873-Q1 15 A</td></tr> </table> | <table><caption> Table 9-15. 问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节说明 VCC3V3_MCU 存在 3V3 过压保护电路：当 VCC3V3 超过 4.03V 时，比较器输出关闭 NMOS 与 PMOS，切断 VCC3V3_SW；EDF 可补充网络与二极管或 TVS 拓扑，但不能据此断言未被文档直接描述的保护能力。
实际回答：是的，3.3V电源有过压保护。 **支撑证据：** 1. **过压保护功能确认：** 设计文档明确将“3V3过压保护电路”列为独立模块，其功能是“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”。[4], [5], [6] 2. **实现方式：** 该过压保护电路通过一个由电压基准和MOS管组成的开关电路实现。具体工作逻辑为：[5] * 当VCC3V3电压低于4.03V时，输出电压正常。 * 当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，电路会断开输出，从而对后级负载起到过压保护作用。[5] **缺失信息：** 无。
评分正文：是的，3.3V电源有过压保护。 **支撑证据：** 1. **过压保护功能确认：** 设计文档明确将“3V3过压保护电路”列为独立模块，其功能是“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”。[4], [5], [6] 2. **实现方式：** 该过压保护电路通过一个由电压基准和MOS管组成的开关电路实现。具体工作逻辑为：[5] * 当VCC3V3电压低于4.03V时，输出电压正常。 * 当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，电路会断开输出，从而对后级负载起到过压保护作用。[5] **缺失信息：** 无。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. 问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：基于现有证据，**无法完整描述以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来**。关键信息缺失如下： 1. **以太网 PHY（U1400）的 1.0V 电源轨的电压精度和电流需求**： - 证据中仅列出了 U1400 的电源引脚（VDDA、VDDIO、VDDMAC、VSLEEP）及其连接的网络（N22956977、N22956687、VSLEEP 等），但**未明确这些网络的电压值**，也未提供 PHY 芯片的 1.0V 电源轨参数（如电压精度、电流需求）。 - 文档中虽有其他电源轨（如 1V8、1V1、0V75）的精度要求，但**未涉及 PHY 的 1.0V 电源**。 2. **从系统电源输入到以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源的完整转换级链**： - 电路中存在网络 **VCC1V0_ETH**（证据[6]），该网络连接至 U1501.OUT（疑似稳压器输出），但： - **U1501 的型号、输入来源、输出能力未提供**，无法判断其是否为 1.0V 电源转换器。 - **VCC1V0_ETH 并未与 PHY 芯片 U1400 的任何电源引脚直接相连**，因此无法确认这是 PHY 的 1.0V 电源。 - U1400 的电源引脚连接的网络（如 VDDA→N22956977、VDDIO→N22956687）**未在现有证据中追溯至任何电源转换级**，系统输入到这些网络的转换路径完全未知。 **结论**：现有证据仅显示存在一个 1.0V 网络（VCC1V0_ETH）和一个 PHY 芯片（U1400），但两者之间无连接关系，且 PHY 的电源参数和转换级链均无数据，因此无法回答该问题。缺失信息包括：PHY 的 1.0V 电源轨电压/电流需求、U1400 各电源引脚的电压域、U1501 的详细规格及其输入来源、VCC1V0_ETH 与 PHY 的关联（如有）。

Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F14</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。 VCC3V3 to VCCOV75 is applied to the kernel power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. TI's scheme TPS62872 is selected.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 采用TPS62872电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75，提供给EQ6核电供电。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 TPS62872 power chip is used to convert VCC3V3 to VCCOV75, which is supplied to EQ nuclear power supply. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：MCU外挂20MHz晶体; The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. 问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：存在证据冲突，无法给出唯一的确定型号。主控 MCU 的型号在不同来源中记录不同，分别列出如下： - **取值 1**：`SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA`（对应型号 `tc367`） **来源**：电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`，实例 U900A、U900B、U900C、U900D、U900E 均标注为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA`（证据 [1]–[5]）。网络 `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8`、`CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2`、`CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5` 中均包含 `MCU` 标识，且与 U900D 引脚相连（证据 [6]–[8]），表明该芯片为主控 MCU。 - **取值 2**：`SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（对应型号 `tc377`） **来源**：文本文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，模块 F01 的描述明确说明“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”（证据 [10]），且模块列表中 F01/MCU 定义为 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（证据 [13]）。 **缺失信息**：无缺失项，但两个来源对同一实体字段给出不同取值，无法确定哪一个是实际设计中的主控 MCU 型号。
评分正文：存在证据冲突，无法给出唯一的确定型号。主控 MCU 的型号在不同来源中记录不同，分别列出如下： - **取值 1**：`SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA`（对应型号 `tc367`） **来源**：电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`，实例 U900A、U900B、U900C、U900D、U900E 均标注为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA`（证据 [1]–[5]）。网络 `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8`、`CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2`、`CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5` 中均包含 `MCU` 标识，且与 U900D 引脚相连（证据 [6]–[8]），表明该芯片为主控 MCU。 - **取值 2**：`SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（对应型号 `tc377`） **来源**：文本文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，模块 F01 的描述明确说明“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”（证据 [10]），且模块列表中 F01/MCU 定义为 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（证据 [13]）。 **缺失信息**：无缺失项，但两个来源对同一实体字段给出不同取值，无法确定哪一个是实际设计中的主控 MCU 型号。

NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F02/F05/F10</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>对EQ6和LPDDR4周边PCB温度进行监控。AOU需求MCU监控EQ6芯片内结温，确保EQ6工作安全。AOU需求EQ6监控CAM的工作温度，要求温度监控功能达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统的功能安全。 The PCB temperature around EQ6 and LPDDR4 was monitored. AOU requires MCU to monitor the junction temperature inside EQ6 chip to ensure the safety of EQ6. AOU requires EQ6 to monitor the operating temperature of the CAM, and the temperature monitoring function is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the functional safety of the EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6温度采样 EQ6-L内部内置了两个温度传感器，并通过外部SPI接口实时发送给MCU，硬件设计可以支持MCU对EQ6结温的监控。 1, EQ6 temperature sampling EQ6-l has two internal temperature sensors, which are sent to the MCU in real time through the external SPI interface. The hardware design can support the MCU to monitor the junction temperature of EQ6. NTC温度采样 硬件设计使用温敏电阻NTC（NCU15XH103F6SRC）采集EQ6/LPDDR4周边环境温度，并通过MCU进行ADC采样和实时监控。NTC温度监控范围：-40~150℃，监控精度±3℃@-40~150℃，MCU的ADC为10bit，即1024个量阶，使得监测精度可以达到3.2mV，电压采集范围0-3.3V。用MCU的AN7引脚采样。 2.NTC temperature sampling In hardware design, the temperature sensor NTC (NCU15XH103F6SRC) is used to collect the ambient temperature of EQ6/LPDDR4, and the ADC sampling and real-time monitoring are carried out by MCU. NTC temperature monitoring range: -40-150℃, monitoring accuracy ±3℃@-40-150℃, MCU ADC is 10bit, that is, 1024 quantity order, so that the monitoring accuracy can reach 3.2mV, voltage acquisition range 0-3.3V. The AN7 pin of the MCU is used for sampling. CAM温度采样 CAM的IMX-728内置了两个温度传感器，温度测量范围是：-40~125℃，温度监控精度为±5℃@-40~125℃。EYEQ6可以通过I2C接口（EQ6L的I2C_0_SCLK和I2C_0_SDA引脚）实时读取IMX-728的工作温度，并进行状态监控和异常诊断。 3.CAM temperature sampling CAM IMX-728 built-in two temperature sensors, temperature measurement range is: -40~125℃, temperature monitoring accuracy of ±5℃@-40~125℃. EYEQ6 can read the operating temperature of the IMX-728 in real time through the I2C interface (I2C_0_SCLK and I2C_0_SDA pins of EQ6L), and perform condition monitoring and anomaly diagnosis. </td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Device Information</caption> <tr><td colspan=3 rowspan=3 >Part Number Current Rating Package TPS62870-Q1 6A TPS62871-Q1 9A RXS (VQFN-FCRLF, TPS62872-Q1 12 A 16) TPS62873-Q1 15 A</td></tr> </table> | <table><caption> Figure 9-22. 问题：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是谁？说清楚输入、三路输出和软件配置出处。
参考答案：U1700 LP87702-Q1 从 VCC3V3 取电，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50，软件配置出处为 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：**板上最主要的电源管理芯片是 LN10046（型号 LN10046FSQ1LQR），为系统一级 DC-DC 转换器。** - **输入**：12V（KL30 系统电源），来源：证据[2]（“将12V系统电源转换为3.3V”）。 - **输出**：单路 3.3V（VCC3V3），来源：证据[2]（“KL30 转 VCC3V3”）。 - **三路输出**：**无直接证据**。LN10046 仅输出一路 3.3V。若问题所指“三路输出”为其他芯片，则 LP87702（二级电源）提供 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50 三路输出，但该芯片并非“最主要的电源管理芯片”。 - **软件配置出处**：**无直接证据**。LN10046 未提及寄存器、I2C 地址等软件配置。文档中软件配置描述（如寄存器、I2C 地址）仅针对 LP87702（I2C 地址 0x08，见证据[8]）和 TPS62872（I2C 地址 0x41，见证据[8]），与 LN10046 无关。 **缺失信息**： - LN10046 的三路输出规格（电压/电流）：证据显示仅有一路 3.3V 输出，无三路输出数据。 - LN10046 的软件配置（寄存器、I2C 地址等）：所有文档均未提供。
评分正文：**板上最主要的电源管理芯片是 LN10046（型号 LN10046FSQ1LQR），为系统一级 DC-DC 转换器。** - **输入**：12V（KL30 系统电源），来源：证据[2]（“将12V系统电源转换为3.3V”）。 - **输出**：单路 3.3V（VCC3V3），来源：证据[2]（“KL30 转 VCC3V3”）。 - **三路输出**：**无直接证据**。LN10046 仅输出一路 3.3V。若问题所指“三路输出”为其他芯片，则 LP87702（二级电源）提供 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50 三路输出，但该芯片并非“最主要的电源管理芯片”。 - **软件配置出处**：**无直接证据**。LN10046 未提及寄存器、I2C 地址等软件配置。文档中软件配置描述（如寄存器、I2C 地址）仅针对 LP87702（I2C 地址 0x08，见证据[8]）和 TPS62872（I2C 地址 0x41，见证据[8]），与 LN10046 无关。 **缺失信息**： - LN10046 的三路输出规格（电压/电流）：证据显示仅有一路 3.3V 输出，无三路输出数据。 - LN10046 的软件配置（寄存器、I2C 地址等）：所有文档均未提供。

Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | None：YYYY年MM月DD日发布 Released on DD/MM/YYYY; 文件编号File No：6XXXXXXXX_BBB_HW_IP_00X 保存期限：产品生命周期加1个日历年；如属安全件，保存15个日历年以上 Retention period:Product life cycle +1 calendar year. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Table 6-1. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. The user cannot select the start-up voltage and I2C address independently.(1) The user can use the VSEL pin to select which of the four start-up voltages the device uses. For more information, see Table 9-5 and (2) 7 Pin Configuration and Functions 8 Specifications 8.1 Absolute Maximum Ratings over operating temperature range (unless otherwise noted)(1) (1)Operation outside the Absolute Maximum Ratings may cause permanent device damage. Absolute Maximum Ratings do not imply functional operation of the device at these or any other conditions beyond those listed under Recommended Operating Conditions. If used outside the Recommended Operating Conditions but within the Absolute Maximum Ratings, the device may not be fully functional, and this may affect device reliability, functionality, performance, and shorten the device lifetime. (3) | Attempting to read data from register addresses not listed in this section will result in Oxoo being read out. 9.5.3 I2c Update Sequence The following are required for a single update: · A start condition · A valid I2C address A register address byte· · A data byte After the receipt of each byte, the receiving device acknowledges by pulling the SDA line low during the high period of a single clock pulse. A valid I2C address selects the target. The target performs an update on the falling edge of the acknowledge signal that follows the LSB byte. "0"Write A = Acknowledge (SDA low) A = Not acknowledge (SDA high) S = START condition Sr = REPEATED START condition P = STOP condition "0" Write I"Read A=Acknowledge(SDAlow) A=Notacknowledge(SDAhigh) S= START condition Sr=REPEATEDSTARTcondition P= STOP condition 9.5.412cRegisterReset The I2C registers can be reset by: Pulling the input voltage below 1.4 V (typical)· Setting the RESET bit in the CONTROL register. When RESET = 1, all registers are reset to their default values and a new start-up begins immediately. After t(En), you can program the I2C registers again. 9.6 Register Map locations. Page Changes from Revision A (July 2022) to Revision B (January 2023) | 9.4.4 0n The device operates in on mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold and all of the following conditions are true: ·A high-level is applied to the EN pin. SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. All functions are available in on mode. The device leaves on mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves on mode and enters standby mode when any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. · The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. 9.5 Programming 9.5.1 Serial Interface Description I2C is a 2-wire serial interface developed by Philips Semiconductor, now NXP Semiconductors (see I2C-Bus Specification and User Manual, Revision 6, 4 April 2014). The bus consists of a data line (SDA) and a clock line (SCL) with pullup structures. When the bus is idle, both SDA and SCL lines are pulled high. All I2C-compatible devices connect to the I2C bus through open-drain I/O pins, SDA and SCL. A controller, usually a microcontroller or a digital signal processor, controls the bus. The controller is responsible for generating the SCL signal and device addresses. The controller also generates specific conditions that indicate the START and STOP of data transfer. A target receives data, transmits data, or both on the bus under control of the controller. The TPS6287x-Q1 device operates as a target and supports the following data transfer modes, as defined in the I2C-Bus Specification: standard mode (100 kbps), fast mode (400 kbps), and fast mode plus (1 Mbps). The interface adds flexibility to the power supply solution, enabling most functions to be programmed to new values depending on the instantaneous application requirements. Register contents remain intact as long as the input voltage remains above 1.4 V. The data transfer protocol for standard and fast modes is exactly the same, therefore, they are referred to as F/S-mode in this document. The device supports 7-bit addressing; general call addresses are not supported.

问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：**结论：** - **输入信号**：VIN (VCC3V3)、EN (N19888813)、GOSNS (GND)、VOSNS (VCC0V75)、COMP (N19809013)、MODE/SYNC (VCC3V3)、VSEL (N19808994)、FSEL (N19808812)、SDA (MCU_SDA_P15.5)、SCL (MCU_SCL_P15.4)。 - **输出检测引脚**：GOSNS、VOSNS（差分输出感应）。 - **使能信号**：EN 引脚，网络为 N19888813。 - **I²C 控制网络**：SDA 接 MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL 接 MCU_SCL_P15.4。 **依据**： - **电路图引脚映射**（证据[1]）显示 U1800 各引脚连接：VIN→VCC3V3，EN→N19888813，GOSNS→GND，VOSNS→VCC0V75，COMP→N19809013，MODE/SYNC→VCC3V3，VSEL→N19808994，FSEL→N19808812，SDA→MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL→MCU_SCL_P15.4。 - **网络连接**（证据[4][5]）确认 MCU_SCL_P15.4 连接 U1800.SCL，MCU_SDA_P15.5 连接 U1800.SDA。 - **数据手册**（证据[7]）说明 GOSNS 为输出接地感应、VOSNS 为输出电压感应，用于输出检测；EN 为使能引脚；SDA/SCL 为 I²C 接口。 **缺失信息**： - 输出检测引脚是否包含 PG（电源良好指示）？证据[1] 中未列出 PG 引脚连接，数据手册虽描述 PG 功能，但无法确认 U1800 实际连接，故不纳入结论。 - 输入信号分类（如电源输入、模拟输入等）未在证据中细化，仅按连接网络列出。

问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过的负载开关是 **U1803（型号 TPS22918）**，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**（来自 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚）。 **支撑证据：** 1. 电路拓扑显示，电源路径为 `VCC1V8 -> U1803 (20181204001), controls: MCU_1V8_EQ_EN -> VCC1V8_EQ`（证据 [1]）。 2. 设计文档描述：“VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” 并明确负载开关型号为 TPS22918（证据 [2] 和 [3]）。 **缺失信息：** 无。
评分正文：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过的负载开关是 **U1803（型号 TPS22918）**，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**（来自 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚）。 **支撑证据：** 1. 电路拓扑显示，电源路径为 `VCC1V8 -> U1803 (20181204001), controls: MCU_1V8_EQ_EN -> VCC1V8_EQ`（证据 [1]）。 2. 设计文档描述：“VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” 并明确负载开关型号为 TPS22918（证据 [2] 和 [3]）。 **缺失信息：** 无。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | None：U20; None：P23.2; None：P23.2/GTM_TIM1_IN6_5/GTM_TIM0_IN6_5/GTM_TOUT43/CAN12_TXD; None：GPIO; None：MCU_RST_FLASH_P23.2; 问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：**直接结论：** 文档声明板上有 **4 个 CAN 相关模块**：CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3。通过网表可以确认每个模块的具体连接，包括差分信号、INH 控制信号、INH 采样信号以及 STB 控制信号等。 **支撑证据：** 1. **文档模块列表**（来源：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，evidence[17]） - 表格明确列出：F20 CAN3、F21 CAN2、F22 CAN1、F23 CAN0。 2. **文档引脚分配**（来源：同上，evidence[18]） - 给出每个 CAN 模块的 MCU 引脚和 SPI 控制信号，如 CAN0 的 STB 信号为 `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8`，CAN1 的 STB 信号为 `CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2` 等。 3. **网表网络**（来源：`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`，evidence[1]–[16]） - 可查到对应网络，例如： - `CAN0H` 连接 C1201.&1、D1200.cathode1、L1200.&4、R1209.&2、R1214.&2、TP1206.&1、X1900.&2。 - `CAN0L` 连接 C1200.&1、D1200.cathode2、L1200.&3、R1212.&2、R1215.&2、TP1215.&1、X1900.&3。 - `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8` 连接 R1126.&1、R1228.&2、R1241.&1、TP1217.&1、U1200.SCSN、U1202.STB、U1203.NSTB。 - 类似地，`CAN1H`、`CAN1L`、`CAN1_INH`、`CAN2H`、`CAN2L`、`CAN2_INH`、`CAN2_INH_ACQ`、`CAN3H`、`CAN3L`、`CAN3_INH`、`CAN3_INH_ACQ` 等网络均存在并连接至特定器件。 **缺失信息：** 无。
评分正文：**直接结论：** 文档声明板上有 **4 个 CAN 相关模块**：CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3。通过网表可以确认每个模块的具体连接，包括差分信号、INH 控制信号、INH 采样信号以及 STB 控制信号等。 **支撑证据：** 1. **文档模块列表**（来源：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，evidence[17]） - 表格明确列出：F20 CAN3、F21 CAN2、F22 CAN1、F23 CAN0。 2. **文档引脚分配**（来源：同上，evidence[18]） - 给出每个 CAN 模块的 MCU 引脚和 SPI 控制信号，如 CAN0 的 STB 信号为 `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8`，CAN1 的 STB 信号为 `CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2` 等。 3. **网表网络**（来源：`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`，evidence[1]–[16]） - 可查到对应网络，例如： - `CAN0H` 连接 C1201.&1、D1200.cathode1、L1200.&4、R1209.&2、R1214.&2、TP1206.&1、X1900.&2。 - `CAN0L` 连接 C1200.&1、D1200.cathode2、L1200.&3、R1212.&2、R1215.&2、TP1215.&1、X1900.&3。 - `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8` 连接 R1126.&1、R1228.&2、R1241.&1、TP1217.&1、U1200.SCSN、U1202.STB、U1203.NSTB。 - 类似地，`CAN1H`、`CAN1L`、`CAN1_INH`、`CAN2H`、`CAN2L`、`CAN2_INH`、`CAN2_INH_ACQ`、`CAN3H`、`CAN3L`、`CAN3_INH`、`CAN3_INH_ACQ` 等网络均存在并连接至特定器件。 **缺失信息：** 无。

When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | 注：使用本模板时请先阅读填写说明页，形成工作产品时本页不可删除变更历史Change History目 录Table of Contents1 概述 Introduction 81.1 目的 Purpose 81.2 适用范围 Range of Application 81.3 读者对象 TReader 81.4 术语及缩写解释 Terms and Abbreviations 81.5 参考文档 Reference Document 82 硬件架构 Hardware Architecture 92.1 系统概述 System overview 92.2 硬件框图 Hardware block diagram 102.3 模块电路 Module circuit 122.3.1 KL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit 122.3.2 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit 132.3.3 电源电路 power circuit 152.3.4 TC377最小系统 TC377 minimum system 232.3.5 EQ6最小系统 EQ6 minimum system 252.3.6 摄像头最小系统 camera minimum system 282.3.7 看门狗电路 Watchdog circuit 302.3.8 温度采样电路 Temp Sample circuit 302.3.9 系统内电源电压采样电源 The power System Voltage Sampling circuit 312.3.10 ETH电路 ETH circuit 332.3.11 CAN/CAN FD电路 CAN/CAN FD circuit 342.3.12 3V3过压保护电路 3V3 overvoltage protection circuit 353 硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode 363.1 硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode 363.2 运行模式定义 Definition of Operation Mode 373.3 运行模式转换条件 Transition Condition of Operation Mode 393.3.1 KL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit 393.3.2 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit 413.3.3 电源电路 Power Circuit 423.3.4 TC377最小系统 TC377 minimum system 53 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR.
