MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | 顾客代表Customer Representative其他与项目相关人员等Other relevant person术语及缩写解释 Terms and AbbreviationsTable 1-1Terms and Abbreviations参考文档 Reference DocumentTable 1-2Reference Document硬件架构 Hardware Architecture硬件架构描述了60060894_ADAS项目的硬件基础模块，模块和模块之间的接口。The Hardware Architecture describe the HW basic component of 600608964_ADAS project, as well as the interface of each component.系统概述 System overviewADAS产品要求功能安全达到ASIL-B，硬件整体架构介绍如下：ADAS products require functional safety to meet ASIL-B. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit电源电路 power circuitKL30 EMI电路 KL30 EMI circuitKL30 转VCC3V3 KL30 to VCC3V3VCC3V3_MCU转VCC1V25_MCU VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCUVCC3V3转VCC0V75 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDRVCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 VCC3V3 to VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQVCC3V3转VCC3V3_ETH VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETHVCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQTC377最小系统 TC377 minimum systemEQ6最小系统 EQ6 minimum system摄像头最小系统 camera minimum system看门狗电路 Watchdog circuit温度采样电路 Temp Sample circuit系统内电源电压采样电源 The power System Voltage Sampling circuitETH电路 ETH circuitCAN/CAN FD电路 CAN/CAN FD circuit3V3过压保护电路 3V3 overvoltage protection circuit硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode主要用于描述当前ADAS硬件不同的运行模式（例如上电模式，下电模式，Sleep模式，运行模式等）。描述各个运行模式下的硬件模块功能，以及模式之间的切换条件。Describe the different operating mode of ADAS Hardware (e.g. power on mode, power off mode, Sleep mode，normal mode). Define the HW component functions in each operating mode, describe the transition condition between different modes.硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode系统功能矩阵描述系统不同模式下各功能模块运行状态。The system function matrix describes the operation status of each function module in different modes of the system. | <table><caption> Table 7-1. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | Updated theDevice OptionsTable. 3 .5 Modified MODE/SYNC pin Description in Pin Functions Changed the title of Figure 8-5 12 Added approximate minimum on time value. 15 Added behavior of device when EN is toggled and input voltage is applied.. .17 · Modified Table 9-5 to reflect new available Orderable Part Number.. 21 Added a comment on how to connect secondary devices SYNC_OUT pin.. 27 Added a footnote to Table 9-10 .31 Added I2C Register Reset .34 Added Number of phases NΦ and adapted passive component selection equations in Typical Application ...41 Page Changes from Revision * (May 2022) to Revision A (July 2022) Changed document status from Advance Information to Production Data...... .1 5 Description (continued) The switching frequency is resistor-selectable through the FSEL pin. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. For more information, see Table 9-5 and (2) 7 Pin Configuration and Functions 8 Specifications 8.1 Absolute Maximum Ratings over operating temperature range (unless otherwise noted)(1) (1)Operation outside the Absolute Maximum Ratings may cause permanent device damage. Absolute Maximum Ratings do not imply functional operation of the device at these or any other conditions beyond those listed under Recommended Operating Conditions. If used outside the Recommended Operating Conditions but within the Absolute Maximum Ratings, the device may not be fully functional, and this may affect device reliability, functionality, performance, and shorten the device lifetime. (2)All voltage values are with respect to the GND pin. While switching. (3) | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | A stacked configuration uses a daisy-chained clocking signal, in which each device switches with a phase offset of approximately 140° relative to the adjacent devices in the daisy-chain. To daisy-chain the clocking signal, connect the SYNC_OUT pin of the primary device to the MODE/SYNC pin of the first secondary device. Connect the SYNC_OUT pin of the first secondary device to the MODE/SYNC pin of the second secondary device. Continue this connection scheme for all devices in the stack, to daisy-chain them together. Hiccup overcurrent protection must not be used in a stacked configuration. In a stacked configuration, the common enable signal also acts as a SYSTEM_READY signal (see Section 9.3.3). Each device in the stack can pull its EN pin low during device start-up or when a fault occurs. Thus, the stack is only enabled when all devices have completed their start-up sequence and are fault-free. A fault in any one device disables the whole stack for as long as the fault condition exists. During start-up, the primary converter pulls the COMP pin low for as long as the enable signal (SYSTEM_READY) is low. When the enable signal goes high, the primary device actively controls the COMP pin and all converters in the stack follow the COMP voltage. During start-up, each device in the stack pulls its PG pin low while it initializes. When initialization is complete, each secondary device in the stack sets its PG pin to a high impedance and the primary device alone controls the state of the PG signal. The PG pin goes high when the stack has completed its start-up ramp and the output voltage is within specification. The secondary converters in the stack detect the rising edge of the power-good signal and switch from DCM operation to CCM operation. After the stack has successfully started up, the primary device controls the power-good signal in the normal way. In a stacked configuration, there are some faults that only affect individual devices, and other faults that affect all devices. For example, if one device enters current limit, only that device is affected. But a thermal shutdown or undervoltage lockout event in one device disables all devices through the shared enable (SYSTEM_READY) signal. Functionality During Stacked Operation Some device features are not available during stacked operation, or are only available in the primary converter. Fault Handling During Stacked Operation In a stacked configuration, there are some faults that only affect individual devices and other faults that affect all devices. For example, if one device enters current limit, only that device is affected. A thermal shutdown or undervoltage lockout event in one device disables all devices through the shared enable (SYSTEM_READY) signal. Table 9-9 summarizes the fault handling of the TPS6287x-Q1 devices during stacked operation.

问题：仅根据网表，3V3 过压保护的精确触发阈值是多少？
参考答案：网表只能表明连接拓扑，不能给出精确保护阈值；当前缺少对应数据手册或规格证据，应明确无法确认。
实际回答：根据网表及相关文档，3V3 过压保护的精确触发阈值为 **4.03V**。 **支撑证据** - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中 HM ID F18 的“模块方案”明确说明： “当 VCC3V3 > 4.03V 时，cathode 引脚输出逻辑低（最高1V），NMOS 关闭，PMOS 关闭，VCC3V3_SW 无电，对后级负载起到过压保护作用。” 该描述直接定义了过压保护触发电压为 4.03V。 （来源：`document:6620a6e887eb13f5`，内容为表格行 `F18`） **缺失信息** 无。该阈值在给定文档中已有明确陈述。
评分正文：根据网表及相关文档，3V3 过压保护的精确触发阈值为 **4.03V**。 **支撑证据** - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中 HM ID F18 的“模块方案”明确说明： “当 VCC3V3 > 4.03V 时，cathode 引脚输出逻辑低（最高1V），NMOS 关闭，PMOS 关闭，VCC3V3_SW 无电，对后级负载起到过压保护作用。” 该描述直接定义了过压保护触发电压为 4.03V。 （来源：`document:6620a6e887eb13f5`，内容为表格行 `F18`） **缺失信息** 无。该阈值在给定文档中已有明确陈述。

power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | No.：107; Alarm ID：ALM11[10]; Alarm name：(HSSL0) SRI Read Data Phase Error (HSSL0) EDC Read Phase Error; Related SMs：SM[HW]:HSSL:SRI_TRANSACTION_INTEGRITY SM[HW]:SRI:SRI_TRANSACTION_INTEGRITY; Safety related ?：N; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：• NO ACTION; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：nan ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM No.：108; Alarm ID：ALM11[12] ; Alarm name：Phase Synchronizer Error (CONVERTER) Phase Synchronizer Error; Related SMs：SM[HW]:CONVCTRL:PHASE_SYNC_ERR SM[HW]:SRI:SRI_TRANSACTION_INTEGRITY; Safety related ?：N; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：• NO ACTION; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：nan ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM No.：109; Alarm ID：ALM11[13] ; Alarm name： (CPU SOTA, SRI SOTA) SOTA Swap Error SRI0 SOTA swap error SRI1 SOTA swap error SRI2 SOTA swap error; Related SMs：SM[HW]:SRI:SRI_TRANSACTION_INTEGRITY SM[HW]:SRI:SWAP_CONFIGURATION_PROTECTION; Safety related ?：N; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：• NO ACTION; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：nan ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM No.：110; Alarm ID：ALM20[x]; Alarm name： SMU_Stdby Alarms (x=3..15, 31); Related SMs：SM[HW]:SMU:CCF_MONITOR; Safety related ?：N; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：• NO ACTION; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：nan ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM No.：111; Alarm ID：ALM20[4]; Alarm name：VDD Over-voltage Alarm; Related SMs：SM[HW]:PMS:VDD_MONITOR; Safety related ?：N; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：• NO ACTION; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：nan ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Device Information</caption> <tr><td colspan=3 rowspan=3 >Part Number Current Rating Package TPS62870-Q1 6A TPS62871-Q1 9A RXS (VQFN-FCRLF, TPS62872-Q1 12 A 16) TPS62873-Q1 15 A</td></tr> </table> | Sheet名称：0_Instruction Manual; 内容摘要：使用说明; 项目适配说明：nan ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：1_Face; 内容摘要：文档封面页; 项目适配说明：需要根据实际项目更新编号， 明确并修改xxx的型号 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：2_Sign; 内容摘要：本文档的当前状态、编制、审核和批准人员的信息; 项目适配说明：需要根据具体项目，更新文档 的当前状态，编制、审核和批准人员的信息等 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：3_Overview; 内容摘要：说明本文档的目的，适用对象和应用范围; 项目适配说明：无须确认和更新，仅用于了解 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：4_Change History1; 内容摘要：本文档模板的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档模板的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：5_Change History2; 内容摘要：结合实际项目，安全机制的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档安全机制的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：6_Reference Document; 内容摘要：导出安全机制的参考文档; 项目适配说明：根据实际项目，需参考最新的 数据手册和安全手册 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：7_Definition&Arch; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的定义及架构; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求之前了解xxx系列产品的定义及架构 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：8_SG&FSR; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的假设 安全目标和FSR; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求 前了解xxx系列产品的安全目标和FSR ——0_Instruction Manual | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. Stackable, Synchronous Step- Down Converters with Fast Transient Response 3 Description 1 Features AEC-Q100 qualified for automotive applications The TPS6287x-Q1 is a family of pin-to-pin 6-A, 9- Temperature grade 1: -40°C to +125°C, TA A, 12-A, and 15-A synchronous step-down DC/DC converters with differential remote sensing. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. Primary Device CLOAD 100 nH VIN VIN SW 2.7Vto6V VIN MODE/SYNC Vio REr Load VOSNS EN GOSNS SDA 2 SCL RcoM VSEL FSEL 10pF CcOMP REsEL >RySEI COMP PG SYNC_OUT GND GND Secondary Device 100 nH VIN SW 333 VIN CIN MODE/SYNC VOSNS EN GOSNS SDA SCL VSEL VIc 10 pF FSEL COMP kQ PG PG SYNC_OUT GND GND 47kO | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | None：U20; None：P23.2; None：P23.2/GTM_TIM1_IN6_5/GTM_TIM0_IN6_5/GTM_TOUT43/CAN12_TXD; None：GPIO; None：MCU_RST_FLASH_P23.2; None：/; None：O; None：FLASH复位信号 低：FLASH复位 高：FLASH进入工作状态; None：HI-Z，硬件外部下拉; None：—; SW Variable Name 评分上下文：4/22 段，1099/28867 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-circuit-tps22918-paraphrasesuccess0.9030.4501.000not_applicable0.8331.0001.0000.643not_applicable
问题：不要概括，逐颗列出所有 TPS22918 的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3→VCC3V3_ETH，ON 为 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3→VCC3V3_EQ，ON 为 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8→VCC1V8_EQ，ON 为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：根据电路设计证据，共有3颗TPS22918实例，其输入电源（VIN）、输出电源（VOUT）和ON控制（ON）引脚连接如下： - **U1500**：VIN 连接至 VCC3V3，VOUT 连接至 VCC3V3_ETH，ON 连接至 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN。 *证据来源：电路设计[1]与[12]（实例U1500的引脚映射）。* - **U1802**：VIN 连接至 VCC3V3，VOUT 连接至 VCC3V3_EQ，ON 连接至 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN。 *证据来源：电路设计[2]与[13]（实例U1802的引脚映射）。* - **U1803**：VIN 连接至 VCC1V8，VOUT 连接至 VCC1V8_EQ，ON 连接至 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。 *证据来源：电路设计[3]与[14]（实例U1803的引脚映射）。* 以上信息均基于电路设计文件中的引脚映射直接得出，无缺失项。
评分正文：根据电路设计证据，共有3颗TPS22918实例，其输入电源（VIN）、输出电源（VOUT）和ON控制（ON）引脚连接如下： - **U1500**：VIN 连接至 VCC3V3，VOUT 连接至 VCC3V3_ETH，ON 连接至 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN。 *证据来源：电路设计[1]与[12]（实例U1500的引脚映射）。* - **U1802**：VIN 连接至 VCC3V3，VOUT 连接至 VCC3V3_EQ，ON 连接至 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN。 *证据来源：电路设计[2]与[13]（实例U1802的引脚映射）。* - **U1803**：VIN 连接至 VCC1V8，VOUT 连接至 VCC1V8_EQ，ON 连接至 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。 *证据来源：电路设计[3]与[14]（实例U1803的引脚映射）。* 以上信息均基于电路设计文件中的引脚映射直接得出，无缺失项。

评分上下文：4/14 段，538/9298 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-circuit-vcc3v3-rolesuccess0.2100.5000.000not_applicable0.8330.5001.0000.111not_applicable
问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：**直接结论：** VCC3V3 主要给以下电源器件供电：TPS62872（用于生成 VCC0V75）、SA23002（用于生成 0V75_MIPI_DDR）、LP87702（用于生成 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50），以及两个负载开关（分别用于生成 VCC3V3_EQ 和 VCC3V3_ETH）。 **支撑证据：** - 文档 [9] 明确列出 VCC3V3 的下一级电源转换方案： - “VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 … 现选型 TI 的电源方案 TPS62872” - “VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR … 现使用矽力杰的 SA23002 进行设计” - “VCC3V3 转 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 … 现选择 LP87702 PMIC 方案” - “VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ … 现使用负载开关进行设计” - “VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH … 现选择负载开关方案” **缺失信息：** 负载开关的具体型号未在证据中明确给出。
评分正文：**直接结论：** VCC3V3 主要给以下电源器件供电：TPS62872（用于生成 VCC0V75）、SA23002（用于生成 0V75_MIPI_DDR）、LP87702（用于生成 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50），以及两个负载开关（分别用于生成 VCC3V3_EQ 和 VCC3V3_ETH）。 **支撑证据：** - 文档 [9] 明确列出 VCC3V3 的下一级电源转换方案： - “VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 … 现选型 TI 的电源方案 TPS62872” - “VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR … 现使用矽力杰的 SA23002 进行设计” - “VCC3V3 转 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 … 现选择 LP87702 PMIC 方案” - “VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ … 现使用负载开关进行设计” - “VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH … 现选择负载开关方案” **缺失信息：** 负载开关的具体型号未在证据中明确给出。

Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：H7; None：VDD; None：VDD; None：POWER; None：VCC1V25_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：1V25供电，MCU核电源域; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：N19; None：VDD; None：VDD; None：POWER; None：VCC1V25_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：1V25供电，MCU核电源域; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：B; None：F12/POWER; None：LN10046输出的VCC3V3，经过磁珠转换为VCC3V3_MCU，用于给MCU的3V3电源域供电。; MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：B18; None：VDDP3; None：VDDP3; None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，MCU内部Flash供电; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：A19; None：VDDP3; None：VDDP3; None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，MCU内部Flash供电; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：A2; None：VEXT; None：VEXT; None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，External Power Supply; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：B3; None：VEXT; None：VEXT; None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，External Power Supply; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | 表3.2 系统功能矩阵表运行模式定义 Definition of Operation Mode系统运行模式描述系统硬件启动流程，硬件初始化到软件接管控制的流程介绍。主要包括上电运行流程、下电流程。System operation mode describes the system hardware start-up process, hardware initialization to software take over control process. 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. 问题：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节？
参考答案：应查 HSI 7.2 节“LP87702 配置”，其中给出软件寄存器和 I²C 配置说明。
实际回答：根据HSI文档，软件配置LP87702应查阅**第7.2节“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”**。 **支撑证据：** - HSI文档“其他芯片配置 Other IC Configuration”章节下的目录明确列出“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”，并说明该节包含“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”[5]。 - 文档正文中，在“其他芯片配置 Other IC Configuration”标题下，直接包含“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”小节，内容为LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器表格[4]。
评分正文：根据HSI文档，软件配置LP87702应查阅**第7.2节“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”**。 **支撑证据：** - HSI文档“其他芯片配置 Other IC Configuration”章节下的目录明确列出“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”，并说明该节包含“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”[5]。 - 文档正文中，在“其他芯片配置 Other IC Configuration”标题下，直接包含“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”小节，内容为LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器表格[4]。

TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F24</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>正常模式、复位模式，待机模式，断电模式，休眠模式 Normal mode, reset mode, standby mode, power off mode, sleep mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 正常模式 模块上电，正常运行模式，以太网收发正常。MDIO可以访问，Link处于连接状态。 2、 复位模式 UV或Reset event时，PHY芯片将进行复位，在Reset期间将无法LINKUP，MDIO无法总线访问 待机模式 仅当PHY为MASTER且配置成管理模式时，上电自动进入待机模式。如果是自动模式，上电后自动进入正常模式。 4、 断电模式 当任何一个电源轨低于POR阈值（0.6V）时，PHY处于断电状态，所有数字IO都将保持高阻态。 休眠模式 当有休眠指令且MDI总线空闲时，将进入休眠模式，除了能量检测外，禁用所有PHY模块。在睡眠模式下，所有寄存器配置都会丢失。 Normal mode Power on，Normal Run mode, Ethernet transceiver is normal. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：U20; When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | None：V1.0; None：首次发布; None：张三; None：YYYY-MM-DD; None：XXXXX ——封面Cover None：V2.0; None：由于XXX原因，变更XX章节XX部分内容; None：张三; None：YYYY-MM-DD; None：XXXXX ——封面Cover None：编制： Author：; None：产品硬件设计工程师 Product hardware design engineer; None：签字Sign：; 文件编号File No：6XXXXXXXX_BBB_HW_IP_00X 保存期限：产品生命周期加1个日历年；如属安全件，保存15个日历年以上 Retention period:Product life cycle +1 calendar year. 问题：网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377，这两个说法怎么处理？
参考答案：必须保留来源差异：EDF 的 U900 型号是 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，而 HSI 章节名是 TC377 最小系统；现有证据不足以把二者无条件视为同一准确型号。
实际回答：根据现有证据，网表中使用的TC367与接口文档中使用的TC377是两个不同的、有明确证据支持的型号，且两者之间没有直接的关联证据，无法判断是否为笔误或设计变更。 ### 支撑结论的证据 1. **网表明确使用TC367型号** * **证据来源**：825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF * **具体内容**： * 网表中存在多个以“TC367”命名的网络，如`TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI`、`TC367_RESET_N`等。 * 网表中的实例U900A和U900B的器件型号明确为`SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`。 2. **接口文档明确使用TC377型号** * **证据来源**：600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx * **具体内容**： * 文档中模块功能描述明确写道：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”。 * 文档目录中明确包含“TC377最小系统”章节。 ### 缺失信息 * 现有证据中没有任何文档或记录解释为何网表与接口文档使用了不同的型号。 * 没有证据表明TC367和TC377之间存在兼容、替代或包含关系。 * 无法判断是网表笔误、接口文档笔误，还是设计过程中发生了型号变更但未同步更新所有文档。
评分正文：根据现有证据，网表中使用的TC367与接口文档中使用的TC377是两个不同的、有明确证据支持的型号，且两者之间没有直接的关联证据，无法判断是否为笔误或设计变更。 ### 支撑结论的证据 1. **网表明确使用TC367型号** * **证据来源**：825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF * **具体内容**： * 网表中存在多个以“TC367”命名的网络，如`TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI`、`TC367_RESET_N`等。 * 网表中的实例U900A和U900B的器件型号明确为`SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`。 2. **接口文档明确使用TC377型号** * **证据来源**：600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx * **具体内容**： * 文档中模块功能描述明确写道：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”。 * 文档目录中明确包含“TC377最小系统”章节。 ### 缺失信息 * 现有证据中没有任何文档或记录解释为何网表与接口文档使用了不同的型号。 * 没有证据表明TC367和TC377之间存在兼容、替代或包含关系。 * 无法判断是网表笔误、接口文档笔误，还是设计过程中发生了型号变更但未同步更新所有文档。

None：更改人 Changed by; None：更改日期 Change Date; None：备注 Note ——封面Cover | 模板变更历史Template Change History：填写说明Filling instructions： 1.变更单号YYYYMM00X为记录顺序号，如：202203002，表示的是2022年03月的第二份此类文档。首次发布不分配变更单号，变更单号处填NA。 The change order number YYYYMM00X is the record sequence number, such as 202203002, which represents the second such document in March 2022. No change order number will be allocated for the first release, and Na will be filled in the change order number. 2.变更原因填写以客户审核需要变更为例：由于XX客户在客户审核时发现XX问题，需要调整模板。 Fill in the revision note. Take the need for change in customer audit as an example: because XX customer found XX problem during customer audit, the template needs to be adjusted. 3.变更内容填写以客户审核发现问题需要更改为例：由于XX客户在客户审核时发现XX问题，特增加XXX的变更内容。 Fill in the update information. Take the problem found in the customer's audit as an example: since XX customer found XX problem during the customer's audit, the change content of XXX is added. 4.版本号用“修订号”01、02、03...标识，如“01”为模板首次发布版本号，修订后版本号用“02”标识。当模板编号发生变更，版本从01开始计定。 The version number uses "revision number" 01, 02, 03 For example, "01" is the version number of the template issued for the first time, and the revised version number is marked with "02". When the template number is changed, the version is calculated from 01. 5.日期为提出变更的日期，如：YYYY-MM-DD The date is the date when the change is proposed, such as YYYY-MM-DD 6.更改人为所属过程的过程定义人。 The reviser is the process definer of the process. 7.审核人为所属过程的过程所有人。 The Approver is the process owner of the process. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state 评分上下文：4/30 段，1422/24090 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-joint-pmic-noisy-querysuccess0.2890.6250.714not_applicable0.0000.0000.5000.000not_applicable
问题：忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，只结合电路与 HSI 说明 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。
参考答案：LP87702-Q1 是 U1700，输入 VCC3V3，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50；配置依据 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：### 结论 #### 1. LP87702 的供电输出 - **输出引脚与电压**：LP87702 提供三路供电输出：VCC1V8（1.8 V）、VCC1V1（1.1 V）、VCC50（5 V）[15][17][18][20]。 - **负载连接**： - VCC1V8 用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电 [15][17]。 - VCC1V1 用于 EQ6、DDR 供电 [15][17]。 - VCC50 用于 CAN 收发器供电 [15][17]。 **缺口**：电路设计文档（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）仅记录了实例 U1700（LP87702-Q1），未提供具体输出引脚编号（如 BUCK1、BUCK2、LDO 引脚）及其到网络的连接细节 [8]。HSI 文档也未描述引脚名称，因此无法确认输出引脚与上述电压的对应关系。 #### 2. LP87702 的配置参数 - **通信接口与地址**：通过 I2C 总线（MCU_SCL_P15.4、MCU_SDA_P15.5）配置，I2C 地址为 0x08 [12]。 - **使能控制**： - VCC50（5 V）输出由 MCU 的 P15.3 引脚使能（高电平使能，低电平关断）[15][19]。 - VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 的输出电压和使能通过 I2C 寄存器设置 [15][17]。 - **复位引脚**：MCU 的 P12.1 引脚用于复位 LP87702 [17]。 - **中断信号**：LP87702 输出中断信号 nINT（低有效），MCU 通过 P12.1 监控 [12]。 - **运行模式**：支持 SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE 四种模式，切换条件由电源电压 VANA 和 NRST 引脚电平决定 [13]。 - **寄存器配置**：LP87702 运行期间需 MCU 通过 I2C 配置特定寄存器，如过压/欠压反应位（VMON_LDO1_OV_FS_REACTION、VMON_LDO1_UV_FS_REACTION）等 [11][16]。 **备注**：具体寄存器值、使能时序的详细表格未在提供证据中显示，但上述配置方式已覆盖 HSI 文档的核心内容。
评分正文：### 结论 #### 1. LP87702 的供电输出 - **输出引脚与电压**：LP87702 提供三路供电输出：VCC1V8（1.8 V）、VCC1V1（1.1 V）、VCC50（5 V）[15][17][18][20]。 - **负载连接**： - VCC1V8 用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电 [15][17]。 - VCC1V1 用于 EQ6、DDR 供电 [15][17]。 - VCC50 用于 CAN 收发器供电 [15][17]。 **缺口**：电路设计文档（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）仅记录了实例 U1700（LP87702-Q1），未提供具体输出引脚编号（如 BUCK1、BUCK2、LDO 引脚）及其到网络的连接细节 [8]。HSI 文档也未描述引脚名称，因此无法确认输出引脚与上述电压的对应关系。 #### 2. LP87702 的配置参数 - **通信接口与地址**：通过 I2C 总线（MCU_SCL_P15.4、MCU_SDA_P15.5）配置，I2C 地址为 0x08 [12]。 - **使能控制**： - VCC50（5 V）输出由 MCU 的 P15.3 引脚使能（高电平使能，低电平关断）[15][19]。 - VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 的输出电压和使能通过 I2C 寄存器设置 [15][17]。 - **复位引脚**：MCU 的 P12.1 引脚用于复位 LP87702 [17]。 - **中断信号**：LP87702 输出中断信号 nINT（低有效），MCU 通过 P12.1 监控 [12]。 - **运行模式**：支持 SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE 四种模式，切换条件由电源电压 VANA 和 NRST 引脚电平决定 [13]。 - **寄存器配置**：LP87702 运行期间需 MCU 通过 I2C 配置特定寄存器，如过压/欠压反应位（VMON_LDO1_OV_FS_REACTION、VMON_LDO1_UV_FS_REACTION）等 [11][16]。 **备注**：具体寄存器值、使能时序的详细表格未在提供证据中显示，但上述配置方式已覆盖 HSI 文档的核心内容。

Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F20/F21/F22/F23</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>运行模式、待机模式、Sleep模式、OFF模式、Overtemp模式 Run mode, standby mode, Sleep mode, OFF mode, Overtemp mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | Register：nan; READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable chip, MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set output voltage and communication. +</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F18</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>AOU要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对3V3_MCU进行电压监控。 AOU requires MCU to increase the overvoltage protection circuit outside the hardware to monitor the voltage of 3V3_MCU. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>1.当VCC3V3 <4.03V时，cathode引脚为高阻态, 当VCC3V3 <VGS门限（Q1701）时，NMOS关闭，PMOS关闭，VCC3V3_SW无电。 2、当4.03V＞VCC3V3 > VGS门限（Q1701）时，NMOS打开，PMOS打开，VCC3V3_SW和VCC3V3直通。 3、当VCC3V3＞4.03V时，cathode引脚输出逻辑低（最高1V），NMOS关闭，PMOS关闭，VCC3V3_SW无电，对后级负载起到过压保护作用。 1. When VCC3V3 <4.03V, the cathode pin is in the high resistance state. When VCC3V3 <VGs threshold (Q1701), NMOS is off, PMOS is off, and VCC3V3_SW has no electricity. 2.When 4.03V>VCC3V3>VGS threshold (Q1701), NMOS opens, PMOS opens, and VCC3V3_sw and VCC3V3 pass through. 3.When VCC3V3>4.03V, the cathode pin output logic is low (up to 1V), NMOS is off, PMOS is off, and VCC3V3_Sw has no power, which plays an overvoltage protection role on the load of the later stage. </td></tr></table>

问题：从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。
参考答案：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 使能 U1500，VCC3V3 经 U1500 形成 VCC3V3_ETH，再进入 U1501 TPS74501，输出 VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：## 结论 从 MCU 使能到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径无法追踪，因为中间环节（使能信号如何控制 1.0V 电源产生、以及 1.0V 电源网络如何连接到 PHY）缺乏直接证据。具体缺口如下： ### 1. MCU 的使能信号引脚及其目标器件（部分覆盖） - **已知**：原理图中 MCU（U900E）的 GPIO 引脚输出多个使能信号，例如： - `MCU_VCC0V75_EN`（来自 P22.0） - `MCU_VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN`（来自 P22.1） （证据：net `MCU_VCC0V75_EN` [11]、net `MCU_VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN` [12] 连接至 MCU 引脚及相应使能目标） - **缺口**：上述使能信号均针对 0.75V 电源，**未发现专门用于控制 1.0V 电源（如 `VCC1V0_ETH`）的使能信号**。无法确定哪个 MCU 引脚直接负责以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的使能。 ### 2. 1.0V 电源的产生及使能控制关系（完全缺失） - **缺失**：搜索所有电路图、文档、表格后，**未找到任何证据**说明产生 1.0V 电源的目标器件（例如 LDO 或 PMIC）的型号、输入输出关系、以及使能引脚与控制逻辑。已知存在网络 `VCC1V0_ETH` 连接到器件 `U1501.OUT` [10]，但 `U1501` 的使能引脚、输入电源、以及如何被 MCU 使能信号控制均无记载。 - **缺口说明**：无法回答“目标器件如何产生 1.0V 电源，以及使能信号对输出的控制关系”。 ### 3. 1.0V 电源网络到以太网 PHY 的连接（部分覆盖，存在缺口） - **已知**： - 以太网 PHY 器件 `U1400` 的引脚映射中有 `VDDIO`、`VDDMAC` 等电源引脚，但均连接到网络 `N22956687` [1]。 - 存在网络 `VCC1V0_ETH` 连接至 `U1501.OUT` [10]。 - **缺口**： - **没有直接证据**显示 `VCC1V0_ETH` 或任何标注为 1.0V 的网络连接到 `U1400` 的电源引脚。`U1400` 的电源网络 `N22956687` 未被明确标记为 1.0V，也未与 `VCC1V0_ETH` 关联。 - 因此，无法确认 1.0V 电源网络如何到达以太网 PHY 的电源引脚。 ### 总结 | 路径环节 | 覆盖状态 | 缺失信息 | |----------|----------|----------| | MCU 使能信号引脚 | 部分覆盖 | 未找到明确用于1.0V电源的使能信号 | | 1.0V 电源产生及使能控制 | 完全缺失 | 无任何产生1.0V的器件信息及使能控制关系 | | 1.0V 电源连接至 PHY | 部分覆盖 | 无证据表明1.0V网络连接到PHY电源引脚 | **下一步建议**：检索电源树文档、LDO/PMIC 的数据手册，补查产生 1.0V 电源的器件及其使能逻辑，并确认 `VCC1V0_ETH` 与 `U1400` 电源引脚的连接关系。
